## 用戶名 (環境設置) · 用法說明

**id**　b42f7b0e642cf0f1fe-2

**english**　Username

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 環境
- 遠程處理服務器

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/environment-settings/username.html#usage-notes

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/environment-settings/username.html

**text**　默認情況下，將不會在 ArcGIS Server 上啟動網格處理服務。 服務器管理員必須首先啟動此服務才能將該服務器用作處理服務器。
要指定處理服務器，請使用服務器的名稱或 IP 地址。 如果處理服務器是服務器集群，請使用為站點主機的服務器名稱。
已獲得授權可以使用網格處理服務器的用戶應是服務器的發布者或管理員。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/environment-settings/username.html#usage-notes

## 045076: 并非所有模型都是使用相同資料創建的。 · 描述

**text**　并非所有在 輸入地統計圖層 參數中提供的地統計圖層都具有相同的資料源。 為了進行公平的模型比較，請使用同一資料源創建所有模型。

## 定義鑲嵌資料集 NoData (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　b45137b1a8fb2b9710-2

**english**　Define Mosaic Dataset NoData

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 鑲嵌資料集工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html

**text**　NoData 可用于定義影像周圍的像素值；然而，如果重新計算覆蓋區以移除這些邊界區域，可以提高鑲嵌資料集的效率。 要重新計算輪廓線，可手動編輯它們或使用 構建輪廓線 工具。
可使用 NoData 值的波段參數指定多個 NoData 值。 在要定義為 NoData 的每個值之間使用空格分隔符。
該工具將在鑲嵌資料集中每個網格項目的函數鏈中插入 掩膜函數。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#usage

## 定義鑲嵌資料集 NoData (資料管理工具) · 參數

**id**　b45137b1a8fb2b9710-6

**english**　Define Mosaic Dataset NoData

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 鑲嵌資料集工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html

**parameter**　bands_for_nodata_value

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- NoData 值的波段 （可選）
- 為每個波段指定 NoData 值。 每個波段都可定義唯一的 NoData 值，也可以針對所有波段使用相同的值。 從下拉列表中選擇波段，然后輸入一個值或多個值。 如果選擇多個 NoData 值，則用空格分隔各值。 如果鑲嵌資料集內每個網格的函數鏈都包含“波段合成”函數，或者如果添加的網格資料的網格類型會向每個網格的函數鏈添加“波段合成”函數，則所有指定的值都將應用于所有波段。 值表列： Band—The band index. NoData Value—The NoData value.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#parameters

## 定義鑲嵌資料集 NoData (資料管理工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　b45137b1a8fb2b9710-14

**english**　Define Mosaic Dataset NoData

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 鑲嵌資料集工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html

**parameter**　bands_for_nodata_value

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- bands_for_nodata_value [band {NoData value},...] （可選）
- 為每個波段或所有波段定義值。 每個波段都可定義唯一的 NoData 值，也可為所有波段指定相同的值。 如果要為選擇的各波段定義多個 NoData 值，則在 bands_for_nodata_value 參數的各 NoData 值之間使用空格分隔符。 該工具插入的掩膜函數會在函數鏈中“波段合成”函數之前插入。 因此，如果鑲嵌資料集內每個網格的函數鏈都包含“波段合成”函數，或者如果添加的網格資料的網格類型會向每個網格的函數鏈添加“波段合成”函數，則所有指定的值都將應用于所有波段。 值表列： Band—The band index. NoData Value—The NoData value.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#derived-output

## 定義鑲嵌資料集 NoData (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　b45137b1a8fb2b9710-22

**english**　Define Mosaic Dataset NoData

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 鑲嵌資料集工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html

**text**　#Specify multiple Nodata values for all bands in one Catalog item

import arcpy
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/Workspace"

mdname = "Nodata.gdb/md"
noofbands = "3"
nodataval = "ALL_BANDS '0 9'"
nodatarange = "#"
query = "OBJECTID=2"
mode = "#"

arcpy.management.DefineMosaicDatasetNoData(mdname, noofbands, nodataval,
                                           nodatarange, query, mode)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#derived-output

## 定義鑲嵌資料集 NoData (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 鑲嵌資料集工具集

**english**　Define Mosaic Dataset NoData

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html

**id**　b45137b1a8fb2b9710-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Define Mosaic Dataset NoData，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。鑲嵌資料集 [in_mosaic_dataset]、波段數 [num_bands]、NoData 值的波段 （可選） [bands_for_nodata_value]、有效資料范圍的波段 （可選） [bands_for_valid_data_range]、查詢定義 （可選） [where_clause]、從每個波段合成 NoData 值 （可選） [Composite_nodata_value]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#parameters

**id**　b45137b1a8fb2b9710-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/define-mosaic-dataset-nodata.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_mosaic_dataset", "label_zh": "鑲嵌資料集", "label_en": "Mosaic Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "The mosaic dataset where you want to update the NoData values."}, {"parameter": "num_bands", "label_zh": "波段數", "label_en": "Number of Bands", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of bands in the mosaic dataset."}, {"parameter": "bands_for_nodata_value", "label_zh": "NoData 值的波段 （可選）", "label_en": "Bands for NoData Value (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Specify the NoData value for each band. Each band can have a unique NoData value defined, or you can use the same value for all bands. Choose the band from the drop-down list and then enter a value or multiple values. If you choose multiple NoData values, separate each value with a space. If the function chain for each raster within the mosaic dataset contains the Composite Bands function, or if your raster data was added with a raster type that adds the Composite Bands function to each raster's function chain, then any value you specify will apply to all bands. Value table columns: Band—The band index. NoData Value—The NoData value."}, {"parameter": "bands_for_valid_data_range", "label_zh": "有效資料范圍的波段 （可選）", "label_en": "Bands For Valid Data Range (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Specify a range of values to display for each band. Values outside of this range will be classified as NoData. When working with composite bands, the range will apply to all bands. Value table columns: Band—The band index. Minimum Value—The minimum value of the data range. Maximum Value—The maximum value of the data range."}, {"parameter": "where_clause", "label_zh": "查詢定義 （可選）", "label_en": "Query Definition (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "SQL Expression", "description_en": "An SQL statement to select specific raster in the mosaic dataset. Only the selected rasters will have their NoData values changed."}, {"parameter": "Composite_nodata_value", "label_zh": "從每個波段合成 NoData 值 （可選）", "label_en": "Composite NoData value from each band (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Choose whether all bands must be NoData in order for the pixel to be classified as NoData. Checked—All of the bands must have pixels of NoData in order for the pixel to be classified as NoData. Unchecked—If any of the bands have pixels of NoData, then the pixel is classified as NoData. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.DefineMosaicDatasetNoData(in_mosaic_dataset, num_bands, {bands_for_nodata_value}, {bands_for_valid_data_range}, {where_clause}, {Composite_nodata_value})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_mosaic_dataset、num_bands、bands_for_nodata_value、bands_for_valid_data_range、where_clause、Composite_nodata_value。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。

工具的名稱

相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 003135：無法同步具有待處理編輯內容的工作空間。 地理資料庫 1 中具有待處理的編輯內容。 · 描述

**id**　b478fd87e9b7164965-1

**english**　003135: Workspaces with pending edits cannot be synchronized. GeoDatabase1 has pending edits.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 3101-3200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03126-03150-003135.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03126-03150-003135.html

**text**　同步復本時，包含復本的工作空間必須處于一致狀態。 如果工作空間中存在待處理的編輯內容，則復本在同步過程后可以不同步。 例如，如果同步的復本含有待處理的編輯內容，且后續已放棄這些編輯，則放棄的編輯內容將不會在下一次同步中傳輸到相關復本。 因此，請在同步復本前保存或放棄地理資料庫 1 中待處理的編輯內容。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-03126-03150-003135.html#description

**id**　b48bb1f02464585316-2

**english**　120391: The input layer has fields with new data types not yet supported by GeoAnalytics: <value>

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告

## 110491：樣本大小小于回歸所需的最小圖徵數 <value>。 · 描述

**id**　b48c23a865130c0482-1

**english**　110491: The sample size is smaller than the minimum number of features required for regression <value>.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110401-110500

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110476-110500-110491.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110476-110500-110491.html

**text**　每棵樹的可用資料 (%) 參數的指定值會導致每棵樹的資料值少于六個。 每棵樹的回歸至少需要六個資料值。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110476-110500-110491.html#description

## 110491：樣本大小小于回歸所需的最小圖徵數 <value>。 · 解決方案

**id**　b48c23a865130c0482-2

**english**　110491: The sample size is smaller than the minimum number of features required for regression <value>.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 110401-110500

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110476-110500-110491.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110476-110500-110491.html

**text**　請增加每棵樹的可用資料 (%) 參數的值，從而使每棵樹至少有六個資料值。 假定系統將保留 10% 的輸入圖徵（向上舍入）以進行驗證。 將剩余圖徵數乘以每棵樹的資料百分比（向下舍入），以確定每棵樹可用的資料值數。
例如，您有 105 個圖徵，而每棵樹有 6% 的可用資料。 在這種情況下，假定系統會保留 11 個值以進行驗證（105 * 10/100 = 10.5，向上舍入為 11），剩下 94 個圖徵可用于訓練。 每棵樹將只有五個資料值，該值小于所需的最小值六（94 * 6/100 = 5.64，向下舍入為 5）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110476-110500-110491.html#solution

## 分析工具集概述 · 分析工具集概述

**id**　b49b4e34b4b98eb211-1

**english**　An overview of the Analysis toolset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/an-overview-of-the-network-analysis-toolset.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/an-overview-of-the-network-analysis-toolset.html

**text**　分析工具集包含用于通過 ArcGIS Network Analyst 擴展模塊執行分析的工具。 該工具集提供了各種工具，包括創建網絡分析圖層并針對隨 Network Analyst 提供的各求解程序設置此圖層的分析屬性的工具、向一個或多個網絡分析類添加網絡分析對象的工具、求解分析問題的工具以及生成駕車方向的工具。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/an-overview-of-the-network-analysis-toolset.html

- 將輸入圖徵或記錄添加到網絡分析圖層。 向特定子圖層（如“停靠點”圖層和“障礙”圖層）添加輸入。 當網絡分析圖層引用網絡資料集作為其網絡資料源時，該工具會計算輸入的網絡位置，除非預先計算的網絡位置欄位是從輸入映射的。

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 分析工具集概述 · 相關工具

**id**　b49b4e34b4b98eb211-31

**english**　An overview of the Analysis toolset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/an-overview-of-the-network-analysis-toolset.html#5C2

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/an-overview-of-the-network-analysis-toolset.html

**text**　使用求解工具執行網絡分析后，可以借助其他地理處理工具利用該結果進行進一步分析。 例如，在通過服務區分析找到一個距離某商店五分鐘車程的多邊形區域范圍后，您可能還需要查找在此區域內居住的特定年齡段的顧客以便郵寄推廣材料。 使用選擇資料工具僅選擇服務區網絡分析圖層中的多邊形區域，再使用按位置選擇圖層工具查找顧客，然后使用復制圖徵工具根據顧客列表創建圖徵類。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/an-overview-of-the-network-analysis-toolset.html#5C2

## 添加車隊路徑 (Network Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-3

**english**　Add Fleet Routing Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**text**　該工具會將路徑添加到具有以路徑名稱前綴參數值開頭的唯一名稱的 Routes 子圖層。
起始站點名稱和終止站點名稱參數值均基于 Depots 子圖層中的名稱。
要刪除所有現有路徑并創建新的路徑，請取消選中追加到現有路徑參數。

- 資料類型

**values**　
- 起始站點名稱 （可選）
- 路徑的起始站點名稱。 如果該值為空，則路徑會將分配的第一個訂單點作為起始點。 車輛的起始位置未知或者與問題不相關時，可以忽略起始站點。 對于車輛配送圖層，如果該值為空，則終止站點名稱參數值不能也為空。 對于最后一公里配送和廢棄物收集圖層，起始站點和終止站點可同時為空。 如果訂單點或站點跨多個時區，則不允許使用虛擬起始站點。
- String

- 標注

- 容量 （可選）
- 車輛的最大收集量（體積、重量、數量等）。 空值等于零。 最多允許 9 個容量欄位，但僅使用對車輛需求進行建模所需的數量。 如果輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層，則此參數不適用且將被隱藏。 值表列： Capacity_1—The first capacity field. Capacity_2—The second capacity field. Capacity_3—The third capacity field. Capacity_4—The fourth capacity field. Capacity_5—The fifth capacity field. Capacity_6—The sixth capacity field. Capacity_7—The seventh capacity field. Capacity_8—The eight capacity field. Capacity_9—The nine capacity field.

## 添加車隊路徑 (Network Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-14

**english**　Add Fleet Routing Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html

**parameter**　route_constraints

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 路徑約束 （可選）
- 將對路徑施加的限制，用于限制總時間、總行駛時間和總距離。 如果輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層，則此參數不適用且將被隱藏。 值表列： Max Total Time—The maximum allowable route duration. The route duration includes travel times as well as service and wait times at orders, depots, and breaks. Specify the value in the input layer's time units. Max Total Travel Time—The maximum allowable travel time for the route. The travel time includes only the time spent driving on the network and does not include service or wait times. This field value can't be larger than the MaxTotalTime field value. Specify the value in the input layer's time units. Max Total Distance—The maximum allowable travel distance for the route. Specify the value in the input layer's distance units.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#parameters

## 添加車隊路徑 (Network Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-15

**english**　Add Fleet Routing Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html

**parameter**　costs

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 成本 （可選）
- 解決方案中路徑可能產生的成本。 如果輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層，則此參數不適用且將被隱藏。 值表列： Fixed Cost—A fixed monetary cost that is incurred only if the route is used in a solution (that is, it has orders assigned to it). Cost Per Unit Time—The monetary cost incurred per unit of work time for the total route duration, including travel times and service and wait times at orders, depots, and breaks. Cost Per Unit Distance—The monetary cost incurred per unit of distance traveled for the route length (total travel distance). Overtime Start Time—The duration of regular work time before overtime computation begins. Cost Per Unit Overtime—The monetary cost incurred per time unit of overtime work. This field can contain null values; a null value indicates that the Cost Per Unit Overtime value is the same as the Cost Per Unit Time value.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#parameters

## 添加車隊路徑 (Network Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-16

**english**　Add Fleet Routing Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html

**parameter**　additional_route_time

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 附加路徑時間 （可選）
- 附件路徑時間選項。 如果輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層，則此參數不適用且將被隱藏。 值表列： Start Depot Service Time—The service time at the starting depot. This can be used to model the time spent loading the vehicle. End Depot Service Time—The service time at the ending depot. This can be used to model the time spent unloading the vehicle. Arrive/Depart Delay—The amount of travel time needed to accelerate the vehicle to normal travel speeds, decelerate it to a stop, and move it off and on the network (for example, in and out of parking). By including an Arrive/Depart Delay value, the solver is deterred from sending many routes to service physically coincident orders.
- Value Table

**parameter**　date_and_time

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 日期和時間 （可選）
- 最后一公里配送圖層的附加日期和時間屬性。 僅當輸入圖層為最后一公里配送圖層時，此參數才適用。 如果輸入圖層具有不同的求解程序類型，則其將被隱藏。 值表列： Earliest Route Start Date—The earliest start date for added routes. If this property is not specified, the routes will use the layer's default earliest route start date. Earliest Route Start Time—The earliest start time of day for added routes. If this property is not specified, the routes will use the layer's default earliest route start time. Route Start Flexibility—Indicates how long after the earliest allowed route start time the route can start. The value can be zero, which means that there is no flexibility in the starting time, or a positive number. Specify the value in the input layer's time units.

- 標注
- 說明

**values**　
- 容量 （可選）
- 廢棄物收集圖層中路徑的容量。 僅當輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層時，此參數才適用。 如果輸入圖層具有不同的求解程序類型，則其將被隱藏。 值表列： MaxBins_1—The maximum number of waste bins for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. MaxWeight_1—The maximum physical weight for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any weight units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all weight fields. MaxVolume_1—The maximum volume for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any volume units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all volume fields. MaxBins_2—The maximum number of waste bins for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. MaxWeight_2—The maximum physical weight for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any weight units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all weight fields. MaxVolume_2—The maximum volume for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any volume units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all volume fields.
- Value Table

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- start_depot_name （可選）
- 路徑的起始站點名稱。 如果該值為空，則路徑會將分配的第一個訂單點作為起始點。 車輛的起始位置未知或者與問題不相關時，可以忽略起始站點。 對于車輛配送圖層，如果該值為空，則 end_depot_name 參數值不能也為空。 對于最后一公里配送和廢棄物收集圖層，起始站點和終止站點可同時為空。 如果訂單點或站點跨多個時區，則不允許使用虛擬起始站點。
- String

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- earliest_start_time （可選）
- 車輛配送圖層中路徑允許的最早開始時間。 求解程序通過將該參數與起始站點的時間窗（在 Depots 子圖層中由 TimeWindowStart 欄位提供）結合使用來確定可行的路徑開始時間。 該參數的默認僅時間值是 8:00:00 a.m.，即為分析圖層 Default Date 屬性提供的日期當天上午 8:00:00。 如果未指定任何值，則將使用默認值。 僅當輸入圖層為車輛配送圖層時，此參數才適用。 如果輸入圖層具有不同的求解程序類型，則將忽略該值。
- Date

- 車輛的最大收集量（體積、重量、數量等）。 空值等于零。 最多允許 9 個容量欄位，但僅使用對車輛需求進行建模所需的數量。 如果輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層，則此參數不適用并且將忽略其值。 值表列： Capacity_1—The first capacity field. Capacity_2—The second capacity field. Capacity_3—The third capacity field. Capacity_4—The fourth capacity field. Capacity_5—The fifth capacity field. Capacity_6—The sixth capacity field. Capacity_7—The seventh capacity field. Capacity_8—The eight capacity field. Capacity_9—The nine capacity field.

## 添加車隊路徑 (Network Analyst 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-34

**english**　Add Fleet Routing Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html

**parameter**　route_constraints

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- route_constraints [route_constraints,...] （可選）
- 將對路徑施加的限制，用于限制總時間、總行駛時間和總距離。 如果輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層，則此參數不適用并且將忽略其值。 值表列： Max Total Time—The maximum allowable route duration. The route duration includes travel times as well as service and wait times at orders, depots, and breaks. Specify the value in the input layer's time units. Max Total Travel Time—The maximum allowable travel time for the route. The travel time includes only the time spent driving on the network and does not include service or wait times. This field value can't be larger than the MaxTotalTime field value. Specify the value in the input layer's time units. Max Total Distance—The maximum allowable travel distance for the route. Specify the value in the input layer's distance units.
- Value Table

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 添加車隊路徑 (Network Analyst 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-35

**english**　Add Fleet Routing Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html

**parameter**　costs

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- costs [costs,...] （可選）
- 解決方案中路徑可能產生的成本。 如果輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層，則此參數不適用并且將忽略其值。 值表列： Fixed Cost—A fixed monetary cost that is incurred only if the route is used in a solution (that is, it has orders assigned to it). Cost Per Unit Time—The monetary cost incurred per unit of work time for the total route duration, including travel times and service and wait times at orders, depots, and breaks. Cost Per Unit Distance—The monetary cost incurred per unit of distance traveled for the route length (total travel distance). Overtime Start Time—The duration of regular work time before overtime computation begins. Cost Per Unit Overtime—The monetary cost incurred per time unit of overtime work. This field can contain null values; a null value indicates that the Cost Per Unit Overtime value is the same as the Cost Per Unit Time value.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#derived-output

## 添加車隊路徑 (Network Analyst 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-36

**english**　Add Fleet Routing Routes

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html

**parameter**　additional_route_time

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- additional_route_time [additional_route_time,...] （可選）
- 附件路徑時間選項。 如果輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層，則此參數不適用并且將忽略其值。 值表列： Start Depot Service Time—The service time at the starting depot. This can be used to model the time spent loading the vehicle. End Depot Service Time—The service time at the ending depot. This can be used to model the time spent unloading the vehicle. Arrive/Depart Delay—The amount of travel time needed to accelerate the vehicle to normal travel speeds, decelerate it to a stop, and move it off and on the network (for example, in and out of parking). By including an Arrive/Depart Delay value, the solver is deterred from sending many routes to service physically coincident orders.
- Value Table

- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- append_to_existing_routes （可選）
- 指定是否將新的路徑追加到現有路徑屬性表中。 APPEND—新的路徑將追加到路徑屬性表的現有集合中。 這是默認設置。 CLEAR—將刪除現有路徑并替換為新路徑。

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-38

**parameter**　date_and_time

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- date_and_time [date_and_time,...] （可選）
- 最后一公里配送圖層的附加日期和時間屬性。 僅當輸入圖層為最后一公里配送圖層時，此參數才適用。 如果輸入圖層具有不同的求解程序類型，則將忽略該值。 值表列： Earliest Route Start Date—The earliest start date for added routes. If this property is not specified, the routes will use the layer's default earliest route start date. Earliest Route Start Time—The earliest start time of day for added routes. If this property is not specified, the routes will use the layer's default earliest route start time. Route Start Flexibility—Indicates how long after the earliest allowed route start time the route can start. The value can be zero, which means that there is no flexibility in the starting time, or a positive number. Specify the value in the input layer's time units.

- 名稱

- 資料類型

- waste_capacities [waste_capacities,...] （可選）
- 廢棄物收集圖層中路徑的容量。 僅當輸入圖層為廢棄物收集圖層時，此參數才適用。 如果輸入圖層具有不同的求解程序類型，則將忽略該值。 值表列： MaxBins_1—The maximum number of waste bins for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. MaxWeight_1—The maximum physical weight for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any weight units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all weight fields. MaxVolume_1—The maximum volume for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any volume units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all volume fields. MaxBins_2—The maximum number of waste bins for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. MaxWeight_2—The maximum physical weight for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any weight units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all weight fields. MaxVolume_2—The maximum volume for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any volume units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all volume fields.

## 添加車隊路徑 (Network Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 分析工具集

**english**　Add Fleet Routing Routes

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html

**id**　b4a036481e5a94eb0c-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Add Fleet Routing Routes，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入車輛配送圖層 [in_vrp_layer]、路徑數 （可選） [number_of_routes]、路徑名稱前綴 （可選） [route_name_prefix]、起始站點名稱 （可選） [start_depot_name]、終止站點名稱 （可選） [end_depot_name]、最早開始時間 （可選） [earliest_start_time]、最晚開始時間 （可選） [latest_start_time]、最大訂單點計數 （可選） [max_order_count]、容量 （可選） [capacities]、路徑約束 （可選） [route_constraints]、成本 （可選） [costs]、附加路徑時間 （可選） [additional_route_time]、追加到現有路徑 （可選） [append_to_existing_routes]、日期和時間 （可選） [date_and_time]、容量 （可選） [waste_capacities]、開始時間 （可選） [start_time]、路徑約束 （可選） [route_time_distance_constraints]、附加路徑時間 （可選） [depot_service_time]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/add-vehicle-routing-problem-routes.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_vrp_layer", "label_zh": "輸入車輛配送圖層", "label_en": "Input Fleet Routing Layer", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Network Analyst Layer", "description_en": "The vehicle routing problem, last mile delivery, or waste collection analysis layer to which routes will be added."}, {"parameter": "number_of_routes", "label_zh": "路徑數 （可選）", "label_en": "Number of Routes", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of routes that will be added."}, {"parameter": "route_name_prefix", "label_zh": "路徑名稱前綴 （可選）", "label_en": "Route Name Prefix", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "A qualifier that will be added to the name of every route. For example, a route name prefix of WeekdayRoute would be used as the starting text for every route's name with an incrementing numerical suffix appended to it (for example, WeekdayRoute1, WeekdayRoute2, and so on)."}, {"parameter": "start_depot_name", "label_zh": "起始站點名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Start Depot Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the starting depot for the route. If this value is null, the route will begin from the first order assigned. Omitting the start depot is useful when the vehicle's starting location is unknown or irrelevant to the problem. For vehicle routing problem layers, when this value is null, the End Depot Name parameter value cannot also be null. Both start and end depots can be null for last mile delivery and waste collection layers. Virtual start depots are not allowed if orders or depots are in multiple time zones."}, {"parameter": "end_depot_name", "label_zh": "終止站點名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "End Depot Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the ending depot for the route. If this value is null, the route will end at the last order assigned. For vehicle routing problem layers, when this value is null, the Start Depot Name parameter value cannot also be null. Both start and end depots can be null for last mile delivery and waste collection layers."}, {"parameter": "earliest_start_time", "label_zh": "最早開始時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Earliest Start Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The earliest allowable start time for the route in a vehicle routing problem layer. This parameter is used by the solver in conjunction with the time window of the starting depot provided in the Depots sublayer by the TimeWindowStart field for determining feasible route start times. This parameter has a default time-only value of 8:00:00 a.m., which is interpreted as 8:00:00 a.m. on the date provided by the Default Date property of the analysis layer. If no value is specified, the default value is used. This parameter is only applicable if the input layer is a vehicle routing problem layer. It is hidden if the input layer has a different solver type."}, {"parameter": "latest_start_time", "label_zh": "最晚開始時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Latest Start Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The latest allowable start time for the route in a vehicle routing problem layer. This parameter has a default time-only value of 10:00:00 a.m., which is interpreted as 10:00:00 a.m. on the date provided by the Default Date property of the analysis layer. If no value is specified, the default value is used. This parameter is only applicable if the input layer is a vehicle routing problem layer. It is hidden if the input layer has a different solver type."}, {"parameter": "max_order_count", "label_zh": "最大訂單點計數 （可選）", "label_en": "Max Order Count (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The maximum allowable number of orders on the route. The default value is 30 for vehicle routing problem layers and null for last mile delivery layers. If no value is specified, the default value is used. This parameter is not applicable and is hidden if the input layer is a waste collection layer."}, {"parameter": "capacities", "label_zh": "容量 （可選）", "label_en": "Capacities (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The maximum amount (volume, weight, quantity, and so on) that can be carried by the vehicle. A null value is the same as zero. A maximum of nine capacity fields are allowed, but use only the number necessary to model the needs of the vehicles. This parameter is not applicable and is hidden if the input layer is a waste collection layer. Value table columns: Capacity_1—The first capacity field. Capacity_2—The second capacity field. Capacity_3—The third capacity field. Capacity_4—The fourth capacity field. Capacity_5—The fifth capacity field. Capacity_6—The sixth capacity field. Capacity_7—The seventh capacity field. Capacity_8—The eight capacity field. Capacity_9—The nine capacity field."}, {"parameter": "route_constraints", "label_zh": "路徑約束 （可選）", "label_en": "Route Constraints (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The constraints that will be placed on routes to limit total time, total travel time, and total distance. This parameter is not applicable and is hidden if the input layer is a waste collection layer. Value table columns: Max Total Time—The maximum allowable route duration. The route duration includes travel times as well as service and wait times at orders, depots, and breaks. Specify the value in the input layer's time units. Max Total Travel Time—The maximum allowable travel time for the route. The travel time includes only the time spent driving on the network and does not include service or wait times. This field value can't be larger than the MaxTotalTime field value. Specify the value in the input layer's time units. Max Total Distance—The maximum allowable travel distance for the route. Specify the value in the input layer's distance units."}, {"parameter": "costs", "label_zh": "成本 （可選）", "label_en": "Costs (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The costs that may be incurred by the route in a solution. This parameter is not applicable and is hidden if the input layer is a waste collection layer. Value table columns: Fixed Cost—A fixed monetary cost that is incurred only if the route is used in a solution (that is, it has orders assigned to it). Cost Per Unit Time—The monetary cost incurred per unit of work time for the total route duration, including travel times and service and wait times at orders, depots, and breaks. Cost Per Unit Distance—The monetary cost incurred per unit of distance traveled for the route length (total travel distance). Overtime Start Time—The duration of regular work time before overtime computation begins. Cost Per Unit Overtime—The monetary cost incurred per time unit of overtime work. This field can contain null values; a null value indicates that the Cost Per Unit Overtime value is the same as the Cost Per Unit Time value."}, {"parameter": "additional_route_time", "label_zh": "附加路徑時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Additional Route Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Additional route time options. This parameter is not applicable and is hidden if the input layer is a waste collection layer. Value table columns: Start Depot Service Time—The service time at the starting depot. This can be used to model the time spent loading the vehicle. End Depot Service Time—The service time at the ending depot. This can be used to model the time spent unloading the vehicle. Arrive/Depart Delay—The amount of travel time needed to accelerate the vehicle to normal travel speeds, decelerate it to a stop, and move it off and on the network (for example, in and out of parking). By including an Arrive/Depart Delay value, the solver is deterred from sending many routes to service physically coincident orders."}, {"parameter": "append_to_existing_routes", "label_zh": "追加到現有路徑 （可選）", "label_en": "Append To Existing Routes (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether new routes will be appended to the existing routes attribute table. Checked—New routes will be appended to the existing set in the routes attribute table. This is the default. Unchecked—Existing routes will be deleted and replaced with new routes."}, {"parameter": "date_and_time", "label_zh": "日期和時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Date and Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Additional date and time properties for a last mile delivery layer. This parameter is only applicable if the input layer is a last mile delivery layer. It is hidden if the input layer has a different solver type. Value table columns: Earliest Route Start Date—The earliest start date for added routes. If this property is not specified, the routes will use the layer's default earliest route start date. Earliest Route Start Time—The earliest start time of day for added routes. If this property is not specified, the routes will use the layer's default earliest route start time. Route Start Flexibility—Indicates how long after the earliest allowed route start time the route can start. The value can be zero, which means that there is no flexibility in the starting time, or a positive number. Specify the value in the input layer's time units."}, {"parameter": "waste_capacities", "label_zh": "容量 （可選）", "label_en": "Capacities (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The capacities for routes in a waste collection layer. This parameter is only applicable if the input layer is a waste collection layer. It is hidden if the input layer has a different solver type. Value table columns: MaxBins_1—The maximum number of waste bins for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. MaxWeight_1—The maximum physical weight for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any weight units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all weight fields. MaxVolume_1—The maximum volume for fraction 1 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any volume units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all volume fields. MaxBins_2—The maximum number of waste bins for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. MaxWeight_2—The maximum physical weight for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any weight units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all weight fields. MaxVolume_2—The maximum volume for fraction 2 that the vehicle can collect before it must be emptied. Any volume units can be used for this value as long as the same units are used for all volume fields."}, {"parameter": "start_time", "label_zh": "開始時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Start Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Additional date and time properties for a waste collection layer. Although you can specify both a date and a time for the Start Time property, only the time portion is used. This parameter is only applicable if the input layer is a waste collection layer. It is hidden if the input layer has a different solver type. Value table columns: Start Time—The starting time for the route. If this property is not specified, the routes will use the layer's default start time. Overtime Start Time—The duration of regular work time before overtime computation begins. A null value indicates that overtime does not apply. Specify the value in the input layer's time units."}, {"parameter": "route_time_distance_constraints", "label_zh": "路徑約束 （可選）", "label_en": "Route Constraints (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The constraints that will be placed on routes to limit total time and distance. This parameter is only applicable if the input layer is a waste collection layer. It is hidden if the input layer has a different solver type. Value table columns: Max Total Time—The maximum allowable route duration. The route duration includes travel times as well as service and wait times at stops, depots, and renewals. Specify the value in the input layer's time units. Max Total Distance—The maximum allowable travel distance for the route. Specify the value in the input layer's distance units."}, {"parameter": "depot_service_time", "label_zh": "附加路徑時間 （可選）", "label_en": "Additional Route Time (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Additional route time properties. This parameter is only applicable if the input layer is a waste collection layer. It is hidden if the input layer has a different solver type. Value table columns: Start Depot Service Time—The service time at the starting depot. This can be used to model the time spent loading the vehicle. End Depot Service Time—The service time at the ending depot. This can be used to model the time spent unloading the vehicle."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.na.AddVehicleRoutingProblemRoutes(in_vrp_layer, number_of_routes, route_name_prefix, {start_depot_name}, {end_depot_name}, {earliest_start_time}, {latest_start_time}, {max_order_count}, {capacities}, {route_constraints}, {costs}, {additional_route_time}, {append_to_existing_routes}, {date_and_time}, {waste_capacities}, {start_time}, {route_time_distance_constraints}, {depot_service_time})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_vrp_layer、number_of_routes、route_name_prefix、start_depot_name、end_depot_name、earliest_start_time、latest_start_time、max_order_count、capacities、route_constraints、costs、additional_route_time、append_to_existing_routes、date_and_time、waste_capacities、start_time、route_time_distance_constraints、depot_service_time。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 概化 (編輯工具) · 注意：

**id**　b4a3ddfe3bfe829f2d-2

**english**　Generalize

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 編輯工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/generalize.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/generalize.html

**text**　此工具會修改輸入資料。 有關詳細信息以及避免意外更改資料的策略，請參閱修改或更新輸入資料的工具。
此工具使用與簡化線工具的點移除方法（道格拉斯-普克簡化算法）相同的算法。 簡化線工具提供了更多的參數，并會創建一個新的輸出，而此工具則直接修改輸入圖徵類。
此工具可按記錄逐一概化圖徵。 運行此工具后，圖徵之間之前重合的線段和面邊界可能不再重合。
貝塞爾曲線段、圓弧段和橢圓弧段將轉換為一組直線段。
此工具不會刪除記錄或圖徵。 如果容差比某個面大，則該面將縮減為三個折點。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/editing/generalize.html#usage

## 概化 (編輯工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　b4a3ddfe3bfe829f2d-14

**english**　Generalize

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 編輯工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/generalize.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/generalize.html

**text**　# Name: BufferZones.py
# Purpose: Simplify features using the Generalize tool and then Buffer them

# Import script modules
import arcpy

# Set the workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data/data.gdb"

# Set local parameters
inFeatures = "zones"
gTolerance = "4 Feet"
copFeatures = "zones_cp"
bufDist = "50 Miles"
bufOutput = "zones_buff"

# Since Generalize permanently updates the input, first make a copy of the
# original feature class
arcpy.management.CopyFeatures(inFeatures, copFeatures)

# Use the Generalize tool to simplify the Buffer input to shorten Buffer
# processing time
arcpy.edit.Generalize(copFeatures, gTolerance)

# Buffer the output
arcpy.analysis.Buffer(copFeatures, bufOutput, bufDist)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/editing/generalize.html#derived-output

## 概化 (編輯工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 編輯工具箱

**english**　Generalize

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/generalize.html

**id**　b4a3ddfe3bfe829f2d-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/editing/generalize.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generalize，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [in_features]、容差 （可選） [tolerance]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 004534：未能將幀添加到時間戳 。 · 描述

**id**　b4a4ccdfaaa87425f6-1

**english**　004534: Failed to add frame at timestamp <value>.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 4501-4600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-04526-04550-004534.html#description

**text**　由于元資料不可讀取、不完整或無效，無法將給定時間戳的幀添加到定向影像資料集中。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-04526-04550-004534.html#description

## 004534：未能將幀添加到時間戳 。 · 解決方案

**id**　b4a4ccdfaaa87425f6-2

**english**　004534: Failed to add frame at timestamp <value>.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 4501-4600

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-04526-04550-004534.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-04526-04550-004534.html

**text**　確認視頻包含有效的幀元資料，并確保嵌入的元資料包含 KLV 必填欄位。
對于 AerialFrame，確認精確時間戳、傳感器緯度、傳感器經度、傳感器橢球體高度或傳感器真實高度、平臺航向角、平臺俯仰角、平臺滾動角、傳感器相對滾動角、傳感器相對高程角、傳感器相對方位角、傳感器水平視域和傳感器垂直視域元資料欄位存在且有效。
對于 TerrestrialFrame 和 Terrestrial360，確認傳感器緯度、傳感器經度、傳感器橢球體高度和精確時間戳元資料欄位存在且有效。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-04526-04550-004534.html#solution

**id**　b4beabd2625e77ce40-9

**english**　Generate Raster From Raster Function

**chapter**

**parameter**　process_as_multidimensional

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 以多維方式處理 （可選）
- 指定是否將輸入鑲嵌資料集作為多維網格資料集進行處理。 選中—輸入將作為多維網格資料集進行處理，并對所有剖切片進行處理以生成新的多維網格資料集。 要使用此選項，請將格式參數設置為 CRF。 未選中—輸入不會作為多維網格資料集進行處理。 如果輸入是多維的，則僅處理當前顯示的切片。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/generate-raster-from-raster-function.html#parameters

- format （可選）
- 輸出網格格式。 默認格式將派生自在 output_raster_dataset 值中指定的文件擴展名。 TIFF—將使用網格資料集的標記圖像文件格式。 Cloud Optimized GeoTIFF—將使用 Cloud Optimized GeoTIFF。 IMAGINE Image—將使用 ERDAS IMAGINE 網格資料格式。 Grid—將使用 ESRI Grid 網格資料集格式。 CRF—將使用云網格格式。 MRF—將使用元網格格式。

**english**　Generate Raster From Raster Function

**chapter**

**parameter**　process_as_multidimensional

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- process_as_multidimensional （可選）
- 指定是否將輸入鑲嵌資料集作為多維網格資料集進行處理。 ALL_SLICES—輸入將作為多維網格資料集進行處理，并對所有剖切片進行處理以生成新的多維網格資料集。 將 format 參數設置為 CRF 以使用此選項。 CURRENT_SLICE—輸入不會作為多維網格資料集進行處理。 如果輸入是多維的，則僅處理當前顯示的切片。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

    raster_function_arguments="Raster \\\\somemachine\\data\\test.tif; VisibleBandID 3;InfraredBandID 4",

## 使用網格函數生成網格 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 網格資料集工具集

**english**　Generate Raster From Raster Function

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-raster-from-raster-function.html

**id**　b4beabd2625e77ce40-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/generate-raster-from-raster-function.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Raster From Raster Function，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格函數 [raster_function]、輸出網格資料集 [out_raster_dataset]、網格函數參數 （可選） [raster_function_arguments]、網格屬性 （可選） [raster_properties]、格式 （可選） [format]、以多維方式處理 （可選） [process_as_multidimensional]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/generate-raster-from-raster-function.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "raster_function", "label_zh": "輸入網格函數", "label_en": "Input Raster Function", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "File; String", "description_en": "The name of a raster function, raster function JSON object, or function chain (in .rft.xml format)."}, {"parameter": "out_raster_dataset", "label_zh": "輸出網格資料集", "label_en": "Output Raster Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The output raster dataset."}, {"parameter": "raster_function_arguments", "label_zh": "網格函數參數 （可選）", "label_en": "Raster Function Arguments (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The parameters associated with the function chain. For example, if the function chain applies the Hillshade raster function, set the data source, azimuth, and altitude. Value table columns: Name—The name of the raster function. Value—The value of the raster function."}, {"parameter": "raster_properties", "label_zh": "網格屬性 （可選）", "label_en": "Raster Properties (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The output raster dataset key properties, such as the sensor or wavelength. Value table columns: Name—The name of the key raster property. Value—The value of the key raster property."}, {"parameter": "format", "label_zh": "格式 （可選）", "label_en": "Format (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The output raster format. The default format will be derived from the file extension specified in the Output Raster Dataset value. TIFF—Tagged Image File Format for raster datasets will be used. Cloud Optimized GeoTIFF—Cloud Optimized GeoTIFF format will be used. ERDAS IMAGINE file—ERDAS IMAGINE raster data format will be used. Esri Grid—Esri Grid raster dataset format will be used. CRF—Cloud Raster Format will be used. MRF—Meta Raster Format will be used."}, {"parameter": "process_as_multidimensional", "label_zh": "以多維方式處理 （可選）", "label_en": "Process as Multidimensional (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the input mosaic dataset will be processed as a multidimensional raster dataset. Checked—The input will be processed as a multidimensional raster dataset and all slices will be processed to produce a new multidimensional raster dataset. Set the Format parameter to CRF to use this option. Unchecked—The input will not be processed as a multidimensional raster dataset. If the input is multidimensional, only the slice that is currently displayed will be processed. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.GenerateRasterFromRasterFunction(raster_function, out_raster_dataset, {raster_function_arguments}, {raster_properties}, {format}, {process_as_multidimensional})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：raster_function、out_raster_dataset、raster_function_arguments、raster_properties、format、process_as_multidimensional。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## TanH (影像分析工具) · 用法

**id**　b4d39946188fe3a729-2

**english**　TanH

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 數學分析工具集
- 三角函數

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/tanh.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/tanh.html

**text**　在數學中，所有三角函數都具有一個確定的有效輸入值范圍，該范圍稱為域。 每個函數中的輸出值也具有一個確定的范圍。 對于此工具，以下表述均為真： 域為：-∞ &lt; [in_value] &lt; ∞ 范圍為：-1 &lt; [out_value] &lt; 1 請注意，-∞ 和 ∞ 分別表示受特定網格格式支持的最小負值和最大正值。
該工具中的輸入和輸出值被看作是無單位量。
無論輸入資料為何種類型，輸出值都始終為浮點型。
如果輸入是多波段網格，則輸出也將為多波段網格。 該工具將在輸入中的每個波段上執行操作。
如果輸入是多維網格資料，則將處理所有變量的所有剖切。 輸出將是 CRF 格式的多維網格。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/tanh.html#usage

**text**　# Name: TanH_Ex_02.py
# Description: Calculates the hyperbolic tangent of pixels in a raster
# Requirements: Image Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.ia import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/iapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inRaster = "degs"

# Run TanH
outTanH = TanH(inRaster)

# Save the output
outTanH.save("C:/iapyexamples/output/outtanh.img")

**source_language**　zh-cn

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

**id**　b4d39946188fe3a729-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/tanh.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 TanH，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網格資料或常量值 [in_raster_or_constant]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

## 向屬性域添加編碼值 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　b4e3dba8d15e8b8427-2

**english**　Add Coded Value To Domain

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性域工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html

**text**　屬性域管理包括以下步驟： 使用 創建屬性域 工具創建屬性域。 使用此工具或 設置范圍屬性域的值 工具添加屬性域的值或為其設置值范圍。 使用 指定欄位的屬性域 工具將屬性域與圖徵類相關聯。
編碼值屬性域既包括存儲在資料庫中的實際值（例如，1 代表硬路面），也包括對編碼值含義的描述（例如，硬路面）。
A coded value domain which specifies a valid set of values for an attribute can apply to any type of attribute—文本、數值、日期等。 例如，文本屬性的編碼值列表可能包括有效的管道材料值：CL - 鑄鐵管；DL - 球墨鑄鐵管；ACP - 石棉混凝土管；或者可能包括表示有效管徑的數值：75–3/4"；2–2"；24–24"；和 30–30"。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html#usage

## 向屬性域添加編碼值 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　b4e3dba8d15e8b8427-18

**english**　Add Coded Value To Domain

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性域工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html

**text**　# Name: MakeDomain.py
# Description: Create an attribute domain to constrain pipe material values

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set the workspace (to avoid having to type in the full path to the data every time)
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data"

# Set local parameters
domName = "Material4"
gdb = "montgomery.gdb"
inFeatures = "Montgomery.gdb/Water/Distribmains"
inField = "Material"

# Process: Create the coded value domain
arcpy.management.CreateDomain("montgomery.gdb", domName, "Valid pipe materials",
                              "TEXT", "CODED")

# Store all the domain values in a dictionary with the domain code as the "key"
# and the domain description as the "value" (domDict[code])
domDict = {"CI":"Cast iron", "DI": "Ductile iron", "PVC": "PVC", \
           "ACP": "Asbestos concrete", "COP": "Copper"}

# Process: Add valid material types to the domain
# use a for loop to cycle through all the domain codes in the dictionary
for code in domDict:
    arcpy.management.AddCodedValueToDomain(gdb, domName, code, domDict[code])

# Process: Constrain the material value of distribution mains
arcpy.management.AssignDomainToField(inFeatures, inField, domName)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html#derived-output

## 向屬性域添加編碼值 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 屬性域工具集

**english**　Add Coded Value To Domain

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html

**id**　b4e3dba8d15e8b8427-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Add Coded Value To Domain，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入工作空間 [in_workspace]、屬性域名稱 [domain_name]、編碼值 [code]、編碼描述 [code_description]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/add-coded-value-to-domain.html#parameters

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。
